Added handlers for zip packet simulation.\",\"diffs\":[{\"target\":\"\",\"replacement\":\"import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from \u0027react\u0027;\\nimport { LogEntry, SystemState, LilyMessage, TTSVoice, ProcessStep, UnitData } from \u0027./types\u0027;\\nimport AuditLog from \u0027./components/AuditLog\u0027;\\nimport ProcessWizard from \u0027./components/ProcessWizard\u0027;\\nimport { LilyAssistant } from \u0027./components/LilyAssistant\u0027;\\nimport { speakMessage } from \u0027./services/geminiService\u0027;\\n\\n// --- REVERT MARKER: V9.1_ZIP_INTEGRATION ---\\n// Added Digital Packet Support \u0026 Auto-Save Triggers\\n// ---------------------------------------------\\n\\nconst INITIAL_LOGS: LogEntry[] \u003d [\\n  { id: 1, msg: \\\"T.H.E.L.M.A. v9.1 System Online\\\", time: new Date().toISOString(), type: \u0027info\u0027 }\\n];\\n\\nconst App \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n  // --- State ---\\n  const [certified, setCertified] \u003d useState\u003cboolean\u003e(true); // Simulating certified driver for demo\\n  const [logs, setLogs] \u003d useState\u003cLogEntry[]\u003e(INITIAL_LOGS);\\n  const [lilyMsg, setLilyMsg] \u003d useState\u003cLilyMessage | null\u003e(null);\\n  const [step, setStep] \u003d useState\u003cProcessStep\u003e(ProcessStep.VERIFICATION);\\n  const [gps, setGps] \u003d useState({ lat: 34.0522, lng: -118.2437 });\\n\\n  // Unit Data State\\n  const [unitData, setUnitData] \u003d useState\u003cUnitData\u003e({\\n    unitId: \u0027\u0027,\\n    photos: { front: false, back: false, left: false, right: false },\\n    bol: \u0027\u0027,\\n    packetUploaded: false,\\n    gpsLocked: true,\\n    timestampStart: new Date().toISOString()\\n  });\\n\\n  // --- Logic Helpers ---\\n\\n  const addLog \u003d (msg: string, type: LogEntry[\u0027type\u0027] \u003d \u0027info\u0027) \u003d\u003e {\\n    const newLog: LogEntry \u003d {\\n      id: Date.now(),\\n      msg,\\n      time: new Date().toISOString(), // High precision timestamp per requirements\\n      type\\n    };\\n    setLogs(prev \u003d\u003e [newLog, ...prev]); \\n  };\\n\\n  const triggerLily \u003d useCallback(async (text: string, mood: \u0027neutral\u0027 | \u0027happy\u0027 | \u0027alert\u0027) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setLilyMsg({ text, mood });\\n    await speakMessage(text, TTSVoice.Kore);\\n    // Auto-dismiss toast after 5s\\n    setTimeout(() \u003d\u003e setLilyMsg(null), 5000);\\n  }, []);\\n\\n  // --- Effects ---\\n  \\n  // GPS Sim\\n  useEffect(() \u003d\u003e {\\n    const interval \u003d setInterval(() \u003d\u003e {\\n      setGps(prev \u003d\u003e ({\\n        lat: prev.lat + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.0005,\\n        lng: prev.lng + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.0005\\n      }));\\n    }, 2000);\\n    return () \u003d\u003e clearInterval(interval);\\n  }, []);\\n\\n  // Auto-Save Trigger on Step Change\\n  useEffect(() \u003d\u003e {\\n    if (step \u003e ProcessStep.VERIFICATION) {\\n        addLog(`SYSTEM_AUTO_SAVE: STATE_CKPT_${step}`, \u0027info\u0027);\\n    }\\n  }, [step]);\\n\\n  // --- Handlers (Thelma Logic) ---\\n\\n  const handleUpdateUnitData \u003d (key: keyof UnitData, value: any) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setUnitData(prev \u003d\u003e ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));\\n  };\\n\\n  const handlePhotoUpload \u003d (side: keyof UnitData[\u0027photos\u0027]) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setUnitData(prev \u003d\u003e ({\\n        ...prev,\\n        photos: { ...prev.photos, [side]: true }\\n    }));\\n    addLog(`Image Captured: ${side.toUpperCase()} VIEW`, \u0027info\u0027);\\n    triggerLily(`Got the ${side} side. clarity looks good.`, \u0027happy\u0027);\\n  };\\n\\n  const handlePacketUpload \u003d async (file: File) \u003d\u003e {\\n    addLog(`UPLINK: INBOUND_PACKET [${(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)}KB]`, \u0027info\u0027);\\n    triggerLily(\\\"Receiving digital route packet. Standby for decompression.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n    \\n    // Simulate processing delay\\n    setTimeout(() \u003d\u003e {\\n        addLog(\\\"DECOMPRESS: manifest.json, hazmat_sheet.pdf, route_auth.sig\\\", \\\"success\\\");\\n        \\n        // Simulate extracting data\\n        setUnitData(prev \u003d\u003e ({ \\n            ...prev, \\n            packetUploaded: true,\\n            bol: \\\"BOL-9844-AX-29\\\" // Auto-filled from \\\"zip\\\"\\n        }));\\n        \\n        triggerLily(\\\"Packet extracted. Bill of Lading data auto-populated.\\\", \\\"happy\\\");\\n        addLog(\\\"HENRY_AGENT: CONTENT_ANALYSIS_COMPLETE\\\", \u0027success\u0027);\\n    }, 2000);\\n  };\\n\\n  const handleNextStep \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n    // Logic Gate based on Step\\n    if (step \u003d\u003d\u003d ProcessStep.VERIFICATION) {\\n        if (!certified) {\\n            triggerLily(\\\"I can\u0027t verify this unit until your certification is renewed.\\\", \u0027alert\u0027);\\n            addLog(\\\"GATE_DENIAL: Operator Uncertified\\\", \u0027error\u0027);\\n            return;\\n        }\\n        addLog(`Unit ${unitData.unitId} HANDSHAKE_CONFIRMED`, \u0027success\u0027);\\n        speakMessage(\\\"Unit Identified. Proceed to condition report.\\\", TTSVoice.Kore);\\n        setStep(ProcessStep.EVIDENCE);\\n    } \\n    else if (step \u003d\u003d\u003d ProcessStep.EVIDENCE) {\\n        addLog(\\\"Condition Report: ALL_ANGLES_SECURED\\\", \u0027success\u0027);\\n        speakMessage(\\\"Evidence secured. Please provide manifest packet.\\\", TTSVoice.Kore);\\n        setStep(ProcessStep.MANIFEST);\\n    }\\n    else if (step \u003d\u003d\u003d ProcessStep.MANIFEST) {\\n        addLog(`Manifest Validated: BOL#${unitData.bol}`, \u0027success\u0027);\\n        addLog(\\\"HENRY_ANALYSIS: WEIGHT_OK | ROUTE_OK\\\", \u0027info\u0027);\\n        speakMessage(\\\"Manifest verified against route limits.\\\", TTSVoice.Fenrir); // Henry speaks\\n        setStep(ProcessStep.SEAL);\\n    }\\n    else if (step \u003d\u003d\u003d ProcessStep.SEAL) {\\n        addLog(\\\"DIGITAL_SEAL_APPLIED. CHAIN_OF_CUSTODY_LOCKED.\\\", \u0027success\u0027);\\n        speakMessage(\\\"Process complete. Drive safe.\\\", TTSVoice.Kore);\\n        setStep(ProcessStep.COMPLETE);\\n    }\\n  };\\n\\n  return (\\n    \u003cdiv className\u003d\\\"min-h-screen bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-900 LogEntry, SystemState, LilyMessage, TTSVoice, ProcessStep, UnitData } from \u0027./types\u0027;\\nimport AuditLog from \u0027./components/AuditLog\u0027;\\nimport ProcessWizard from \u0027./components/ProcessWizard\u0027;\\nimport { LilyAssistant } from \u0027./components/LilyAssistant\u0027;\\nimport { speakMessage } from \u0027./services/geminiService\u0027;\\n\\n// --- REVERT MARKER: V9.2_MAIN_DASHBOARD_EXPANSION ---\\n// Added Command Center Dashboard \u0026 System Navigation\\n// --------------------------------------------------\\n\\nconst INITIAL_LOGS: LogEntry[] \u003d [\\n  { id: 1, msg: \\\"T.H.E.L.M.A. v9.2 System Online\\\", time: new Date().toISOString(), type: \u0027info\u0027 }\\n];\\n\\nconst App \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n  // --- System State ---\\n  const [view, setView] \u003d useState\u003c\u0027DASHBOARD\u0027 | \u0027WORKFLOW\u0027\u003e(\u0027DASHBOARD\u0027);\\n  \\n  // --- Workflow State ---\\n  const [certified, setCertified] \u003d useState\u003cboolean\u003e(true);\\n  const [logs, setLogs] \u003d useState\u003cLogEntry[]\u003e(INITIAL_LOGS);\\n  const [lilyMsg, setLilyMsg] \u003d useState\u003cLilyMessage | null\u003e(null);\\n  const [step, setStep] \u003d useState\u003cProcessStep\u003e(ProcessStep.VERIFICATION);\\n  const [gps, setGps] \u003d useState({ lat: 34.0522, lng: -118.2437 });\\n\\n  // Unit Data State\\n  const [unitData, setUnitData] \u003d useState\u003cUnitData\u003e({\\n    unitId: \u0027\u0027,\\n    photos: { front: false, back: false, left: false, right: false },\\n    bol: \u0027\u0027,\\n    packetUploaded: false,\\n    gpsLocked: true,\\n    timestampStart: new Date().toISOString()\\n  });\\n\\n  // --- Logic Helpers ---\\n\\n  const addLog \u003d (msg: string, type: LogEntry[\u0027type\u0027] \u003d \u0027info\u0027) \u003d\u003e {\\n    const newLog: LogEntry \u003d {\\n      id: Date.now(),\\n      msg,\\n      time: new Date().toISOString(),\\n      type\\n    };\\n    setLogs(prev \u003d\u003e [newLog, ...prev]); \\n  };\\n\\n  const triggerLily \u003d useCallback(async (text: string, mood: \u0027neutral\u0027 | \u0027happy\u0027 | \u0027alert\u0027) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setLilyMsg({ text, mood });\\n    await speakMessage(text, TTSVoice.Kore);\\n    setTimeout(() \u003d\u003e setLilyMsg(null), 5000);\\n  }, []);\\n\\n  // --- Effects ---\\n\\n  // Initial Welcome\\n  useEffect(() \u003d\u003e {\\n    // Small delay to allow UI to render before speaking\\n    const timer \u003d setTimeout(() \u003d\u003e {\\n        triggerLily(\\\"Welcome back, Operator. Base44 Command Center is ready.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n    }, 1000);\\n    return () \u003d\u003e clearTimeout(timer);\\n  }, [triggerLily]);\\n  \\n  // GPS Sim\\n  useEffect(() \u003d\u003e {\\n    const interval \u003d setInterval(() \u003d\u003e {\\n      setGps(prev \u003d\u003e ({\\n        lat: prev.lat + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.0005,\\n        lng: prev.lng + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.0005\\n      }));\\n    }, 2000);\\n    return () \u003d\u003e clearInterval(interval);\\n  }, []);\\n\\n  // Auto-Save Trigger on Step Change\\n  useEffect(() \u003d\u003e {\\n    if (view \u003d\u003d\u003d \u0027WORKFLOW\u0027 \u0026\u0026 step \u003e ProcessStep.VERIFICATION) {\\n        addLog(`SYSTEM_AUTO_SAVE: STATE_CKPT_${step}`, \u0027info\u0027);\\n    }\\n  }, [step, view]);\\n\\n  // --- Navigation Handlers ---\\n\\n  const handleNavigate \u003d (target: \u0027DASHBOARD\u0027 | \u0027WORKFLOW\u0027) \u003d\u003e {\\n    if (target \u003d\u003d\u003d \u0027WORKFLOW\u0027) {\\n        triggerLily(\\\"Initiating Unit Intake Protocol. Please verify driver certification.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n    } else {\\n        triggerLily(\\\"Returning to Command Center.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n    }\\n    setView(target);\\n  };\\n\\n  // --- Workflow Handlers ---\\n\\n  const handleUpdateUnitData \u003d (key: keyof UnitData, value: any) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setUnitData(prev \u003d\u003e ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));\\n  };\\n\\n  const handlePhotoUpload \u003d (side: keyof UnitData[\u0027photos\u0027]) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setUnitData(prev \u003d\u003e ({\\n        ...prev,\\n        photos: { ...prev.photos, [side]: true }\\n    }));\\n    addLog(`Image Captured: ${side.toUpperCase()} VIEW`, \u0027info\u0027);\\n    triggerLily(`Got the ${side} side. Clarity looks good.`, \u0027happy\u0027);\\n  };\\n\\n  const handlePacketUpload \u003d async (file: File) \u003d\u003e {\\n    addLog(`UPLINK: INBOUND_PACKET [${(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)}KB]`, \u0027info\u0027);\\n    triggerLily(\\\"Receiving digital route packet. Standby for decompression.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n    \\n    setTimeout(() \u003d\u003e {\\n        addLog(\\\"DECOMPRESS: manifest.json, hazmat_sheet.pdf, route_auth.sig\\\", \\\"success\\\");\\n        setUnitData(prev \u003d\u003e ({ \\n            ...prev, \\n            packetUploaded: true,\\n            bol: \\\"BOL-9844-AX-29\\\" \\n        }));\\n        triggerLily(\\\"Packet extracted. Bill of Lading data auto-populated.\\\", \\\"happy\\\");\\n        addLog(\\\"HENRY_AGENT: CONTENT_ANALYSIS_COMPLETE\\\", \u0027success\u0027);\\n    }, 2000);\\n  };\\n\\n  const handleNextStep \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n    if (step \u003d\u003d\u003d ProcessStep.VERIFICATION) {\\n        if (!certified) {\\n            triggerLily(\\\"I can\u0027t verify this unit until your certification is renewed.\\\", \u0027alert\u0027);\\n            addLog(\\\"GATE_DENIAL: Operator Uncertified\\\", \u0027error\u0027);\\n            return;\\n        }\\n        addLog(`Unit ${unitData.unitId} HANDSHAKE_CONFIRMED`, \u0027success\u0027);\\n        speakMessage(\\\"Unit Identified. Proceed to condition report.\\\", TTSVoice.Kore);\\n        setStep(ProcessStep.EVIDENCE);\\n    } \\n    else if (step \u003d\u003d\u003d ProcessStep.EVIDENCE) {\\n        addLog(\\\"Condition Report: ALL_ANGLES_SECURED\\\", \u0027success\u0027);\\n        speakMessage(\\\"Evidence secured. Please provide manifest packet.\\\", TTSVoice.Kore);\\n        setStep(ProcessStep.MANIFEST);\\n    }\\n    else if (step \u003d\u003d\u003d ProcessStep.MANIFEST) {\\n        addLog(`Manifest Validated: BOL#${unitData.bol}`, \u0027success\u0027);\\n        addLog(\\\"HENRY_ANALYSIS: WEIGHT_OK | ROUTE_OK\\\", \u0027info\u0027);\\n        speakMessage(\\\"Manifest verified against route limits.\\\", TTSVoice.Fenrir);\\n        setStep(ProcessStep.SEAL);\\n    }\\n    else if (step \u003d\u003d\u003d ProcessStep.SEAL) {\\n        addLog(\\\"DIGITAL_SEAL_APPLIED. CHAIN_OF_CUSTODY_LOCKED.\\\", \u0027success\u0027);\\n        speakMessage(\\\"Process complete. Drive safe.\\\", TTSVoice.Kore);\\n        setStep(ProcessStep.COMPLETE);\\n    }\\n  };\\n\\n  // --- Sub-Renderers ---\\n\\n  const renderDashboard \u003d () \u003d\u003e (\\n    \u003cdiv className\u003d\\\"max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full p-6 space-y-8 animate-fade-in\\\"\u003e\\n      {/* Welcome Banner */}\\n      \u003cdiv className\u003d\\\"bg-indigo-900 rounded-3xl p-8 lg:p-12 text-white relative overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border border-indigo-700\\\"\u003e\\n         \u003cdiv className\u003d\\\"absolute top-0 right-0 w-96 h-96 bg-indigo-500 rounded-full mix-blend-overlay filter blur-3xl opacity-30 -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/4\\\"\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\\n         \u003cdiv className\u003d\\\"absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-64 h-64 bg-purple-500 rounded-full mix-blend-overlay filter blur-3xl opacity-20 translate-y-1/2 -translate-x-1/4\\\"\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\\n         \\n         \u003cdiv className\u003d\\\"relative z-10\\\"\u003e\\n           \u003cdiv className\u003d\\\"flex items-center gap-3 mb-4 opacity-80\\\"\u003e\\n                \u003cdiv className\u003d\\\"w-2 h-2 bg-green-400 rounded-full animate-pulse\\\"\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\\n                \u003cspan className\u003d\\\"text-xs font-mono font-bold tracking-widest uppercase\\\"\u003eSystem Online\u003c/span\u003e\\n           \u003c/div\u003e\\n           \u003ch2 className\u003d\\\"text-4xl md:text-5xl font-black mb-4 tracking-tight\\\"\u003eCommand Center\u003c/h2\u003e\\n           \u003cp className\u003d\\\"text-indigo-200 text-lg mb-8 max-w-2xl leading-relaxed\\\"\u003e\\n             Good Morning, Operator Mike. setLogs(prev \u003d\u003e [newLog, ...prev]); \\n  };\\n\\n  const triggerLily \u003d useCallback(async (text: string, mood: \u0027neutral\u0027 | \u0027happy\u0027 | \u0027alert\u0027) \u003d\u003e Added a stopSpeech helper.\",\"diffs\":[{\"target\":\"\",\"replacement\":\"import { GoogleGenAI, Modality } from \\\"@google/genai\\\";\\nimport { TTSVoice } from \\\"../types\\\";\\n\\nconst API_KEY \u003d process.env.API_KEY || \u0027\u0027;\\n\\n// Singleton for AudioContext and current source to prevent overlaps\\nlet audioContext: AudioContext | null \u003d null;\\nlet currentSource: AudioBufferSourceNode | null \u003d null;\\n\\nconst getAudioContext \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n  if (!audioContext) {\\n    audioContext \u003d new (window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext)({\\n      sampleRate: 24000,\\n    });\\n  }\\n  return audioContext;\\n};\\n\\nexport const stopSpeech \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n  if (currentSource) {\\n    try {\\n      currentSource.stop();\\n    } catch (e) {\\n      // Source might have already finished\\n    }\\n    currentSource \u003d null;\\n  }\\n};\\n\\n// Helper: Base64 decoding\\nfunction decodeBase64(base64: string): Uint8Array {\\n  const binaryString \u003d atob(base64);\\n  const len \u003d binaryString.length;\\n  const bytes \u003d new Uint8Array(len);\\n  for (let i \u003d 0; i \u003c len; i++) {\\n    bytes[i] \u003d binaryString.charCodeAt(i);\\n  }\\n  return bytes;\\n}\\n\\n// Helper: Decode Audio Data (PCM to AudioBuffer)\\nasync function decodeAudioData(\\n  data: Uint8Array,\\n  ctx: AudioContext,\\n  sampleRate: number \u003d 24000,\\n  numChannels: number \u003d 1\\n): Promise\u003cAudioBuffer\u003e {\\n  const dataInt16 \u003d new Int16Array(data.buffer);\\n  const frameCount \u003d dataInt16.length / numChannels;\\n  const buffer \u003d ctx.createBuffer(numChannels, frameCount, sampleRate);\\n\\n  for (let channel \u003d 0; channel \u003c numChannels; channel++) {\\n    const channelData \u003d buffer.getChannelData(channel);\\n    for (let i \u003d 0; i \u003c frameCount; i++) {\\n      channelData[i] \u003d dataInt16[i * numChannels + channel] / 32768.0;\\n    }\\n  }\\n  return buffer;\\n}\\n\\nexport const speakMessage \u003d async (text: string, voice: TTSVoice \u003d TTSVoice.Kore): Promise\u003cvoid\u003e \u003d\u003e {\\n  if (!API_KEY) {\\n    console.error(\\\"Gemini API Key missing.\\\");\\n    return;\\n  }\\n\\n  // Stop any currently playing audio before starting new ones\\n  stopSpeech();\\n\\n  try {\\n    const ai \u003d new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey: API_KEY });\\n    \\n    // Using the Kore voice as the unified system voice\\n    const response \u003d await ai.models.generateContent({\\n      model: \\\"gemini-2.5-flash-preview-tts\\\",\\n      contents: [{ parts: [{ text: text }] }],\\n      config: {\\n        responseModalities: [Modality.AUDIO], \\n        speechConfig: {\\n          voiceConfig: {\\n            prebuiltVoiceConfig: { voiceName: TTSVoice.Kore },\\n          },\\n        },\\n      },\\n    });\\n\\n    const base64Audio \u003d response.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.inlineData?.data;\\n\\n    if (!base64Audio) return;\\n\\n    const ctx \u003d getAudioContext();\\n    if (ctx.state \u003d\u003d\u003d \u0027suspended\u0027) {\\n      await ctx.resume();\\n    }\\n\\n    const audioBytes \u003d decodeBase64(base64Audio);\\n    const audioBuffer \u003d await decodeAudioData(audioBytes, ctx);\\n\\n    const source \u003d ctx.createBufferSource();\\n    source.buffer \u003d audioBuffer;\\n    \\n    const gainNode \u003d ctx.createGain();\\n    gainNode.gain.value \u003d 1.0; \\n\\n    source.connect(gainNode);\\n    gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);\\n    \\n    source.start();\\n    currentSource \u003d source;\\n\\n    source.onended \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n      if (currentSource \u003d\u003d\u003d source) {\\n        currentSource \u003d null;\\n      }\\n    };\\n\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error(\\\"Error generating speech:\\\", error);\\n  }\\n};\"}]},{\"path\":\"App.tsx\",\"generationType\":1,\"isDone\":true,\"description\":\"Unified all agent audio feedback through Lily\u0027s voice. Set \u0027certified\u0027 to true by default to prevent blocking \u0027Launch\u0027 access. Enhanced dashboard visuals for a more complete \u0027system\u0027 feel.\",\"diffs\":[{\"target\":\"\",\"replacement\":\"import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from \u0027react\u0027;\\nimport { LogEntry, LilyMessage, TTSVoice, ProcessStep, UnitData, HenryAnalysis } from \u0027./types\u0027;\\nimport AuditLog from \u0027./components/AuditLog\u0027;\\nimport ProcessWizard from \u0027./components/ProcessWizard\u0027;\\nimport { LilyAssistant } from \u0027./components/LilyAssistant\u0027;\\nimport { speakMessage, stopSpeech } from \u0027./services/geminiService\u0027;\\n\\n// --- T.H.E.L.M.A. DEMONSTRATION SYSTEM KERNEL ---\\n// Access Unblocked: certified \u003d true\\n// Audio Unified: All voices through Lily (Kore)\\n// -----------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nconst INITIAL_LOGS: LogEntry[] \u003d [\\n  { id: 1, msg: \\\"T.H.E.L.M.A. Governance Engine Initialized\\\", time: new Date().toISOString(), type: \u0027info\u0027, agent: \u0027THELMA\u0027 },\\n  { id: 2, msg: \\\"Mode: DEMONSTRATION (Full Access Granted)\\\", time: new Date().toISOString(), type: \u0027success\u0027, agent: \u0027THELMA\u0027 }\\n];\\n\\nconst App \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n  // --- System State ---\\n  const [view, setView] \u003d useState\u003c\u0027DASHBOARD\u0027 | \u0027WORKFLOW\u0027\u003e(\u0027DASHBOARD\u0027);\\n  \\n  // --- Workflow State ---\\n  // Defaulting to TRUE so \u0027Launch\u0027 is not blocked\\n  const [certified, setCertified] \u003d useState\u003cboolean\u003e(true); \\n  \\n  const [logs, setLogs] \u003d useState\u003cLogEntry[]\u003e(INITIAL_LOGS);\\n  const [lilyMsg, setLilyMsg] \u003d useState\u003cLilyMessage | null\u003e(null);\\n  const [step, setStep] \u003d useState\u003cProcessStep\u003e(ProcessStep.VERIFICATION);\\n  const [gps, setGps] \u003d useState({ lat: 34.0522, lng: -118.2437 });\\n  const [henryState, setHenryState] \u003d useState\u003cHenryAnalysis\u003e({\\n    status: \u0027IDLE\u0027,\\n    details: { route: \u0027Pending\u0027, weather: \u0027Pending\u0027, compliance: \u0027Pending\u0027 }\\n  });\\n\\n  // Unit Data State\\n  const [unitData, setUnitData] \u003d useState\u003cUnitData\u003e({\\n    unitId: \u0027\u0027,\\n    photos: { front: false, back: false, left: false, right: false },\\n    bol: \u0027\u0027,\\n    packetUploaded: false,\\n    gpsLocked: true,\\n    timestampStart: new Date().toISOString()\\n  });\\n\\n  // --- Logic Helpers ---\\n\\n  const addLog \u003d (msg: string, type: LogEntry[\u0027type\u0027] \u003d \u0027info\u0027, agent: LogEntry[\u0027agent\u0027] \u003d \u0027THELMA\u0027) \u003d\u003e {\\n    const newLog: LogEntry \u003d {\\n      id: Date.now(),\\n      msg,\\n      time: new Date().toISOString(),\\n      type,\\n      agent\\n    };\\n    setLogs(prev \u003d\u003e [newLog, ...prev]); \\n  };\\n\\n  const triggerLily \u003d useCallback(async (text: string, mood: \u0027neutral\u0027 | \u0027happy\u0027 | \u0027alert\u0027) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setLilyMsg({ text, mood });\\n    // Unified voice: Kore\\n    await speakMessage(text, TTSVoice.Kore);\\n    setTimeout(() \u003d\u003e setLilyMsg(null), 6000);\\n  }, []);\\n\\n  // --- Effects ---\\n\\n  // Initial Welcome\\n  useEffect(() \u003d\u003e {\\n    const timer \u003d setTimeout(() \u003d\u003e {\\n        triggerLily(\\\"Welcome to the T.H.E.L.M.A. Command Center. All systems are nominal and ready for inspection.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n    }, 1000);\\n    return () \u003d\u003e clearTimeout(timer);\\n  }, [triggerLily]);\\n  \\n  // GPS Sim\\n  useEffect(() \u003d\u003e {\\n    const interval \u003d setInterval(() \u003d\u003e {\\n      setGps(prev \u003d\u003e ({\\n        lat: prev.lat + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.0005,\\n        lng: prev.lng + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.0005\\n      }));\\n    }, 2000);\\n    return () \u003d\u003e clearInterval(interval);\\n  }, []);\\n\\n  // Governance Checks (Explainability)\\n  useEffect(() \u003d\u003e {\\n    if (view \u003d\u003d\u003d \u0027WORKFLOW\u0027 \u0026\u0026 step \u003e ProcessStep.VERIFICATION) {\\n        addLog(`GOVERNANCE_AUTO_LOG: STEP_${step} VALIDATED`, \u0027success\u0027, \u0027THELMA\u0027);\\n    }\\n  }, [step, view]);\\n\\n  // --- Actions ---\\n\\n  const handleSimulateTraining \u003d async () \u003d\u003e {\\n    addLog(\\\"TRAINING_MODULE: \u0027Advanced Hazmat Inspection\u0027 Refresh\\\", \u0027warning\u0027, \u0027LILY\u0027);\\n    triggerLily(\\\"Reviewing safety protocols. Training is always integrated into operations.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n  };\\n\\n  const handleNavigate \u003d (target: \u0027DASHBOARD\u0027 | \u0027WORKFLOW\u0027) \u003d\u003e {\\n    stopSpeech();\\n    if (target \u003d\u003d\u003d \u0027WORKFLOW\u0027) {\\n        triggerLily(\\\"Opening Gate Operations Module. System check passed.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n    } else {\\n        triggerLily(\\\"Returning to Command Overview.\\\", \\\"neutral\\\");\\n    }\\n    setView(target);\\n  };\\n\\n  const handleUpdateUnitData \u003d (key: keyof UnitData, value: any) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setUnitData(prev \u003d\u003e ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));\\n  };\\n\\n  const handlePhotoUpload \u003d (side: keyof UnitData[\u0027photos\u0027]) \u003d\u003e {\\n    setUnitData(prev \u003d\u003e ({\\n        ...prev,\\n        photos: { ...prev.photos, [side]: true }\\n    }));\\n    addLog(`IMAGE_SECURED: ${side.toUpperCase()}`, \u0027info\u0027, \u0027THELMA\u0027);\\n    triggerLily(`Captured the ${side} profile. Visual validation confirmed.`, \u0027happy\u0027);\\n  };\\n\\n  const handlePacketUpload \u003d async (file: File) \u003d\u003e {\\n    addLog(`INGESTING: ${file.name}`, \u0027info\u0027, \u0027THELMA\u0027);\\n    triggerLily(\\\"Analyzing route packet. currentSource.stop();\\n    } catch (e) {\\n      // Source might have already finished\\n    }\\n    currentSource \u003d null;\\n  }\\n};\\n\\n// Helper: Base64 decoding (manual implementation required by guidelines)\\nfunction decodeBase64(base64: string): Uint8Array {\\n  const binaryString \u003d atob(base64);\\n  const len \u003d binaryString.length;\\n  const bytes \u003d new Uint8Array(len);\\n  for (let i \u003d 0; i \u003c len; i++) {\\n    bytes[i] \u003d binaryString.charCodeAt(i);\\n  }\\n  return bytes;\\n}\\n\\n// Helper: Decode Audio Data (PCM to AudioBuffer)\\nasync function decodeAudioData(\\n  data: Uint8Array,\\n  ctx: AudioContext,\\n  sampleRate: number \u003d 24000,\\n  numChannels: number \u003d 1\\n): Promise\u003cAudioBuffer\u003e {\\n  const dataInt16 \u003d new Int16Array(data.buffer);\\n  const frameCount \u003d dataInt16.length / numChannels;\\n  const buffer \u003d ctx.createBuffer(numChannels, frameCount, sampleRate);\\n\\n  for (let channel \u003d 0; channel \u003c numChannels; channel++) {\\n    const channelData \u003d buffer.getChannelData(channel);\\n    for (let i \u003d 0; i \u003c frameCount; i++) {\\n      channelData[i] \u003d dataInt16[i * numChannels + channel] / 32768.0;\\n    }\\n  }\\n  return buffer;\\n}\\n\\n// speakMessage uses gemini-2.5-flash-preview-tts to convert text to speech\\nexport const speakMessage \u003d async (text: string, voice: TTSVoice \u003d TTSVoice.Kore): Promise\u003cvoid\u003e \u003d\u003e {\\n  // Stop any currently playing audio before starting new ones\\n  stopSpeech();\\n\\n  try {\\n    // Initializing GoogleGenAI with process.env.API_KEY directly inside the call to avoid stale keys\\n    const ai \u003d new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey: voiceConfig: {\\n            prebuiltVoiceConfig: { voiceName: voice },\\n          },\\n        },\\n      },\\n    });\\n\\n    const base64Audio \u003d response.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.inlineData?.data;\\n\\n    if (!base64Audio) return;\\n\\n    const ctx \u003d getAudioContext();\\n    if (ctx.state \u003d\u003d\u003d \u0027suspended\u0027) {\\n      await ctx.resume();\\n    }\\n\\n    const audioBytes \u003d decodeBase64(base64Audio);\\n    const audioBuffer \u003d await decodeAudioData(audioBytes, ctx);\\n\\n    const source \u003d ctx.createBufferSource();\\n    source.buffer \u003d audioBuffer;\\n    \\n    const gainNode \u003d ctx.createGain();\\n    gainNode.gain.value \u003d 1.0; \\n\\n    source.connect(gainNode);\\n    gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);\\n    \\n    source.start();\\n    currentSource \u003d source;\\n\\n    source.onended \u003d () \u003d\u003e {\\n      if (currentSource \u003d\u003d\u003d source) {\\n        currentSource \u003d null;\\n      }\\n    };\\n\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error(\\\"Error generating speech:\\\", error);\\n  }\\n};\\n\"}]}]},\"createdTimestamp\":{\"seconds\":1768007459,\"nanos\":958000000},\"doneTimestamp\":{\"seconds\":1768007463,\"nanos\":84000000}},{\"id\":184,\"author\":\"user\",\"payload\":{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Review
